It produces written specs, not visuals and not code. ([beeuxd/bee-design-harness](https://github.com/beeuxd/bee-design-harness/blob/HEAD/harness-core/agents/architect.md))
- **architect**: Architect. Advises the desk on cross-cutting design, arbitrates when two specialists disagree, guards the invariants that keep the pipeline one-directional, and records every settled decision as an ADR in docs/decisions/. Use before writing code for a new module, when a change touches more than one owner's files, when two agents have reached different answers, or when someone proposes changing src/fbe/types.py. ([beezhub/fundamental-bias-engine](https://github.com/beezhub/fundamental-bias-engine/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/architect.md))
- **architect**: Prend les décisions de design système qui engagent le projet sur la durée. - **architect**: Plans multi-change OpenSpec workflows. Doubles as a "super explore" that delegates to `superpowers-brainstorming` when the input is vague. Read-only with one exception — writes only under `openspec/workflows/**`. ([benni-arnarsson/ormstunga](https://github.com/benni-arnarsson/ormstunga/blob/HEAD/.agents/agents/architect.md))
